# Third-party notices

SlideForge's own code is licensed under the Apache License 2.0 (see `LICENSE`
and `NOTICE`). Third-party packages retain their own copyrights and license
terms; this document does not relicense them.

## Scope and provenance

The inventory below is generated from the checked-in `package-lock.json` and
the **actually installed** package manifests. Production entries are installed
lockfile packages not marked `dev`, including optional platform packages present
on this Linux installation and packages shared with development tooling.
Versions are not inferred from npm's latest tags. Development-only packages are
listed separately; they are not all included in the browser bundle.

`dist/exporters/engine.js` is a Vite bundle containing `dom-to-pptx`, `pptxgenjs`,
and transitive DOM/HTML/image/font tooling. To avoid relying on minifier-retained
comments, this file preserves complete available production license/NOTICE
texts, including bundled third-party attribution files. It is intentionally
broader than the exact tree-shaken browser output. The package's published-file
list includes this document, so the full notices travel with the exporter.

Installed license files are reproduced verbatim, not replaced with just SPDX
labels. A package without its own license file is explicitly identified below;
where available, an upstream license is retrieved at the npm release's
`gitHead`, or an installed companion package from the same release supplies the
notice. `isarray` includes its complete license in its README; that section is
preserved. No implementation source files are copied.

The `https@1.0.0` npm package contains only a manifest declaring ISC and no
implementation or full license text in this installation. Its declaration is
recorded, not fabricated into a copyright notice. Recheck this and any missing
notice information before shipping a different package/bundle configuration.

Chromium downloaded by Playwright, system libraries, installed fonts, and user
images are separate artifacts with their own notices and redistribution rights;
they are not relicensed by SlideForge or included in this npm inventory.
The included MIT/ISC/BSD/Apache/MPL/dual-license terms must be respected when
redistributing applicable material. In particular, do not assume the entire
dependency graph is MIT-only, and retain applicable MPL source obligations when
redistributing covered binaries.

Refresh this document when the lockfile or bundled package versions change.
The tables and full texts below are a snapshot, not a general compliance
certificate for future versions or external assets.

Since this snapshot, `dom-to-pptx` has moved from `dependencies` to
`devDependencies`: Node never loads it, and the build bundles it into
`dist/exporters/engine.js`. Installing the npm package therefore no longer
installs `dom-to-pptx`, Puppeteer, or their other dependencies, although the
tables below still list them as production entries. Their texts are kept,
because the published bundle contains code from `dom-to-pptx` and its browser
dependencies.
